It’s more that most days lately I’ve had this heavy, low feeling, like I’m just getting through work and home with everything kind of dimmed down.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any periods in the past eleven months where you felt even more depressed than you do now?\n\nPatient: Not really a separate time that was clearly worse. It’s more been this ongoing thing lately—most days I feel pretty low and heavy, like I’m just going through work and home and everything feels kind of muted.\n\nDoctor: Are there any activities that you still enjoy or look forward to?\n\nPatient: Not much, honestly. Not crying all the time or anything, but it’s like everything feels kind of heavy and muted, and I’m just getting through work and home without much feeling behind it.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been feeling this way?\n\nPatient: Looking back, it’s been off and on for about eleven months. The low feeling and guilt haven’t been constant every day, but they keep coming back.\n\nDoctor: Have you lost interest in activities that you used to enjoy?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I have. I usually look forward to things like grilling on the weekend, going fishing, or just putting on a record, but lately I just kind of leave all that stuff sitting there. I don’t really feel like doing it, or care much either way.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced any periods where you felt completely unable to take care of things or make decisions?\n\nPatient: Not completely unable, no. I’m still getting to work and handling the basics, but I’ve had to double-check myself more than usual. And I’ve pulled back from friends and family some, because I don’t really trust my mood or my judgment lately.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any thoughts of death or suicide?\n\nPatient: I haven’t made any kind of plan to hurt myself. But… yeah, sometimes I catch myself thinking it might be easier if I just didn’t wake up one morning.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced any significant changes in your appetite or sleep patterns lately?\n\nPatient: Yeah, my appetite’s been off. I’m mostly just grabbing a few bites here and there, and some nights I skip supper altogether. I’ve noticed my belt’s a little looser too, even though I’m not trying to lose weight.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any periods where your low mood was significantly worse than it has been lately?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if I’d say there’s been a separate period that was worse. It’s more that most days lately I’ve had this heavy, low feeling, like I’m just getting through work and home with everything kind of dimmed down.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any periods in the past eleven months where you felt even more depressed than you do now?\n\nPatient: Not really a separate time that was clearly worse. It’s more been this ongoing thing lately—most days I feel pretty low and heavy, like I’m just going through work and home and everything feels kind of muted.\n\nDoctor: Are there any activities that you still enjoy or look forward to?\n\nPatient: Not much, honestly. I used to look forward to grilling on the weekend, maybe going fishing, or just putting on a record. Lately the stuff just sits there and I don’t really feel like bothering with it.\n\nDoctor: Are there any activities that you still find pleasure in?\n\nPatient: Not really. I mean, I still think about grilling or fishing sometimes, or putting on a record, but when it comes down to it I just leave the stuff sitting there. I don’t really care enough to do it lately.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're unable to experience pleasure from activities that would normally bring you happiness?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I think so. The things I’d normally enjoy—grilling on the weekend, fishing, putting on a record—I just don’t really get anything out of them lately. Most of the time I leave the stuff sitting there and don’t care enough to start.\n\nDoctor: Would you say your low mood is consistently worse in the morning or is it fairly steady throughout the day?\n\nPatient: It’s pretty steady through the day, I think. Most days lately I’ve just had this heavy, low feeling, like I’m going through work and then home with the color turned down. It doesn’t seem especially tied to the morning.\n\nDoctor: Are there any activities that you still enjoy or find pleasure in?\n\nPatient: Not much, no. I usually look forward to things like grilling on the weekend, going fishing, or just putting on a record, but lately I just kind of leave all that stuff sitting there. I don’t really feel like doing it, or care much either way.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced any periods where you felt completely unable to take care of things or make decisions?\n\nPatient: Not completely unable, no. I’m still getting to work and handling the basics, but I’ve had to double-check myself more than usual. And I’ve pulled back from friends and family some, because I don’t really trust my mood or my judgment lately.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any thoughts of death or suicide?\n\nPatient: I haven’t made any kind of plan to hurt myself. But… yeah, sometimes I catch myself thinking it might be easier if I just didn’t wake up one morning.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced any significant changes in your appetite or sleep patterns lately?\n\nPatient: Yeah, my appetite’s been off. I’m mostly just grabbing a few bites here and there, and some nights I skip supper altogether. I’ve noticed my belt’s a little looser too, even though I’m not trying to lose weight.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any periods where your low mood was significantly worse than it has been lately?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if I’d say there’s been a separate period that was worse. It’s more that most days lately I’ve had this heavy, low feeling, like I’m just getting through work and home with everything kind of dimmed down.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any periods in the past eleven months where you felt even more depressed than you do now?\n\nPatient: Not really a separate time that was clearly worse. It’s more been this ongoing thing lately—most days I feel pretty low and heavy, like I’m just going through work and home and everything feels kind of muted.\n\nDoctor: Are there any activities that you still enjoy or look forward to?\n\nPatient: Not much, honestly. I used to look forward to grilling on the weekend, maybe going fishing, or just putting on a record. Lately the stuff just sits there and I don’t really feel like bothering with it.\n\nDoctor: Are there any activities that you still find pleasure in?\n\nPatient: Not really. I mean, I still think about grilling or fishing sometimes, or putting on a record, but when it comes down to it I just leave the stuff sitting there. I don’t really care enough to do it lately.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're unable to experience pleasure from activities that would normally bring you happiness?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I think so. The things I’d normally enjoy—grilling on the weekend, fishing, putting on a record—I just don’t really get anything out of them lately. Most of the time I leave the stuff sitting there and don’t care enough to start.\n\nDoctor: Would you say your low mood is consistently worse in the morning or is it fairly steady throughout the day?\n\nPatient: It’s pretty steady through the day, I think. Most days lately I’ve just had this heavy, low feeling, like I’m going through work and then home with the color turned down. It doesn’t seem especially tied to the morning.\n\nDoctor: Are there any activities that you still enjoy or find pleasure in?\n\nPatient: Not much, no. Like I said, I usually look forward to grilling on the weekend, or going fishing, or even just putting on a record, but lately I mostly just leave the stuff sitting there. I don’t really care enough to do it.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you've lost interest in things you used to enjoy more than you feel like you no longer get pleasure from them?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I think it’s more that I’ve lost interest. I usually look forward to grilling on the weekend, going fishing, or putting on a record, but lately I just kind of leave it all sitting there. I don’t really care enough to start.\n\nDoctor: Would you describe your current low mood as mild, moderate, or severe?\n\nPatient: I’d call it mild. It’s there most days, that heavy, kind of low feeling, like I’m just moving through work and home with the color turned down, but I’m still getting through things.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing.
